if(/environment|agri|forest/.test(k))return 'environment'; if(/media|digital|internet/.test(k))return 'media'; return scene; } function enrichQ(q,candidate){ if(!q||seenQ.has(q))return;seenQ.add(q); const cat=category(q.description),original=String(q.description||'').trim(),key=candidate+'|'+q.date+'|'+original; } function observeGameWindow(){ const gw=document.getElementById('game_window');if(!observer||!gw)return; observer.observe(gw,{childList:true,subtree:true}); } function scheduleDecorate(){ if(!document||decorationPending)return; '; } function cvHexRgb(hex){const h=String(hex||'#888').replace('#','');const v=h.length===3?h.split('').map(x=>x+x).join(''):h.padEnd(6,'8').slice(0,6);return [parseInt(v.slice(0,2),16),parseInt(v.slice(2,4),16),parseInt(v.slice(4,6),16)];} function cvMixColor(base,target,t){const a=cvHexRgb(base),b=cvHexRgb(target),x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));return '#'+a.map((v,i)=>Math.round(v+(b[i]-v)*x).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');} function cvStateColor(shares){ const rows=Object.entries(shares||{}).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);if(!rows.length)return '#d5d5d5'; const leader=rows[0][0],share=rows[0][1]||0,runner=rows[1]?.[1]||0,margin=Math.max(0,share-runner); // Native-TCT-style visual hierarchy: close states are pale; dominant leads become saturated. const strength=Math.max(.30,Math.min(.96,.30+(share-.25)*.55+margin*1.65)); return cvMixColor('#f2f3f4',COLORS[leader]||'#888',strength); } function issueWord(v,pos,neg,mid='Mixed'){return v>=2?pos:v<=-1?neg:mid;} function stateSummaryHTML(uf,res=null){ const st=CV_STATE_DATA[uf],meta=CV_STATE_SUMMARIES[uf]||{headline:st.name,blurb:'A state campaign with its own arithmetic.',focus:'Local organization'};const i=st.issues||{},s=CV2014.state; const chips=[['Development',issueWord(i.dev,'Public investment','Fiscal restraint','Pragmatic')],['Social policy',issueWord(i.social,'Expansion','Skeptical','Mixed')],['Markets',issueWord(i.market,'Market-oriented','State-led','Balanced')],['Reform',issueWord(i.reform,'Reformist','Machine politics','Mixed')],['Environment',issueWord(i.environment,'Green priority','Development first','Secondary')]]; return `

Brazil, 2014

A campaign conducted inside a country that had stopped agreeing on what the previous twelve years meant.

The election this mod is about

Dilma Rousseff entered 2014 as the first woman to hold Brazil's presidency, heir to twelve years of PT government and favorite for reelection. The economy was slowing, inflation had returned to everyday conversation, the June 2013 demonstrations were still politically unresolved and Petrobras was moving from symbol of national development toward scandal.

Aécio Neves offered the PSDB its best route back to the Planalto since 2002. Eduardo Campos tried to break the PT–PSDB cycle from the center-left. After Campos died in a plane crash on 13 August, Marina Silva replaced him and, for several weeks, appeared capable of replacing the entire structure of the election with him.

The first round on 5 October produced Dilma at 41.60%, Aécio at 33.55% and Marina at 21.32%. Three weeks later Dilma defeated Aécio by 51.64% to 48.36% — 54,501,118 votes to 51,041,155. The presidential result was close. The Congress elected alongside it was not simple at all.

What Coração Valente changes

The mod treats 2014 as contingent rather than a prologue whose ending everyone already knows. The player can preserve the historical contest, break the runoff entirely, produce a Marina presidency, return the PSDB to power or create results large enough to distort the congressional and state races around them.

The congressional figures in the endings are estimates, not claims that presidential vote share mechanically converts into seats. Brazil elects the Chamber proportionally and the Senate and governors in separate state races.

JUNE 2013The demonstrations remain in the room

Public transport protests expand into a national argument over services, representation, corruption and the political system.

13 AUG 2014Eduardo Campos dies

The PSB presidential candidate dies in a plane crash in Santos. Marina Silva is registered as his replacement nine days later.

5 OCT 2014The first round

Dilma leads; Aécio overtakes Marina for second place. The TSE mathematically identifies the runoff at 19:56 with 91% of valid votes counted.

26 OCT 201451.64 to 48.36

Dilma wins one of the tightest presidential runoffs of the democratic era, while a highly fragmented Congress waits for the victor.

`, reading:`

Further reading

The election makes more sense when the campaign, the institutions and the political system are read together.

TSE — Final first-round result

The official first-round totals, electorate and legal proclamation of the Dilma–Aécio runoff.

TSE — Definitive second-round result

Official final totals: 54,501,118 votes for Dilma and 51,041,155 for Aécio.

TSE — Marina replaces Eduardo Campos

The legal and political mechanics of one of the most consequential candidate substitutions in Brazilian presidential history.

Chamber of Deputies — party benches elected in 2014

The institutional reality underneath every presidential mandate: PT 69, PMDB 65, PSDB 54, and 513 seats divided among many parties.

Senado Notícias — the Senate after 2014

PMDB remained the largest Senate party; PT and PSDB followed, while PSB emerged larger.

Borba, Veiga & Martins — negative campaigning

A historical study of negative presidential advertising from 1989 through 2014 and why second rounds encourage it.

TSE — Elections 2014 statistical report

Electorate, accessibility, biometric voting and operational statistics from the election itself.

TSE — first-round operational data

Turnout, blank and null votes, biometric identification and the unusually fast national count.

`, sources:`

Data & sources

What is historical, what is simulated and what is deliberately estimated.

Historical baseline

Official presidential vote totals and electorate figures come from the Tribunal Superior Eleitoral. Historical Chamber and Senate party sizes use the post-election figures published by the Câmara dos Deputados and Senado Federal. State boundaries are simplified from IBGE geography.

The real 2014 Chamber contained 513 deputies. PT elected 69, PMDB 65, PSDB 54, PP 38, PSD 36, PR 34 and PSB 34. The Senate entering 2015 remained led by PMDB, followed by PT and PSDB.

Counterfactual warning

The ending's congressional and gubernatorial numbers are scenario estimates. They move with unusually strong or weak presidential performances, but they are intentionally constrained because legislative and state elections are separate contests.

A 60% runoff landslide does not retroactively turn sixty percent of the Chamber into the winner's party.

The mod's political narrative is interpretive. Vote totals, dates and historical institutional baselines are sourced; alternate-history coalition numbers and future paths are authored estimates designed to stay plausible within the campaign the player created.
`, facts:`

Fun facts

The actual election was already strange enough before the alternate history starts.

142.8mBrazilian voters were eligible in 2014.

The electorate listed by the TSE contained 142,822,046 voters.

52.13%of the electorate was female.

Brazil had 74,459,424 registered women voters — fitting for an election led by the country's first woman president.

19:56was enough to know the runoff.

On first-round night, the TSE mathematically identified the second round at 19:56:28 with 91% of valid votes counted — then a record.

21.7mvoters were eligible for biometric identification.

Of them, 91.5% were successfully identified by fingerprint according to the TSE.

8polling sections used manual voting.

Out of 434,340 electronic voting machines used in the first round, only eight sections nationwide resorted to paper/manual voting.

9 daysseparated Eduardo Campos's death and Marina's registration.

Campos died on 13 August; the PSB coalition filed Marina Silva and Beto Albuquerque's replacement ticket on 22 August.

3,459,963valid votes separated Dilma and Aécio.

The final national totals were 54,501,118 to 51,041,155.

28parties elected at least one federal deputy.

The 513-seat Chamber was fragmented enough that even a strong presidential mandate still required coalition construction.

';const select=host.querySelector('select');select.value=selectedUF;select.onchange=ev=>ask(ev.target.value,ev.target);} } function stateScheduleHTML(){ const h=CV2014.state.visitHistory||[]; return `

Field schedule

The campaign stops for a mandatory visit after every two questions, from question 2 through question 34. Pick a state, then pick a city. The city changes the mix of organization, media and constituency effects. Mandatory travel does not spend campaign cash.

`:'

No mandatory visits completed yet.

'}`; } // Largest-remainder allocation gives integer ballots and preserves each UF total. function ballotRows(res){ return Object.entries(res.states).map(([uf,shares],i)=>{ const total=Math.round(CV_STATE_DATA[uf].valid),rows=Object.entries(shares).map(([candidate,p])=>({candidate,votes:Math.floor(total*p),fraction:total*p-Math.floor(total*p)})); let remaining=total-rows.reduce((n,c)=>n+c.votes,0);[...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.fraction-a.fraction).forEach(c=>{if(remaining>0){c.votes++;remaining--;}}); return {uf,total,index:i,rows}; }); } function countingSnapshot(count,step){ const national={},states={};let reported=0,total=0; count.ballots.forEach(st=>{ const delay=st.index%7,ratio=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(step-delay)/(48-delay))); const rows=st.rows.map((c,i)=>({candidate:c.candidate,votes:step>=48?c.votes:Math.floor(c.votes*Math.pow(ratio,.72+((st.index*3+i*7)%13)/15))})); const votes=rows.reduce((n,c)=>n+c.votes,0);rows.forEach(c=>national[c.candidate]=(national[c.candidate]||0)+c.votes); rows.sort((a,b)=>b.votes-a.votes);states[st.uf]={rows,votes,total:st.total,called:votes>0&&rows[0].votes>(rows[1]?.votes||0)+(st.total-votes)}; reported+=votes;total+=st.total; }); return {states,national,reported,total,percent:100*reported/total,complete:step>=48}; } function stopCount(){if(electionCount?.timer)global.clearTimeout(electionCount.timer);if(electionCount)electionCount.timer=null;} function renderCount(){ const c=electionCount;if(!c||!c.root.isConnected)return;const snap=countingSnapshot(c,c.step);c.snapshot=snap; const rank=Object.entries(snap.national).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]); c.root.querySelector('#progress_text').textContent=`${snap.percent.toFixed(1)}% counted · ${fmt(snap.reported)} of ${fmt(snap.total)} valid votes`; c.root.querySelector('#cv2014-count-progress').value=snap.percent; c.root.querySelector('#top_bar_list').innerHTML=rank.map(([k,v])=>`
